Protocol: 1) "PHASE 1: Parse [prd-path] [tdd-path?]" → analyze → present task breakdown → AskUserQuestion (approve/modify). 2) "PHASE 2: Approved breakdown: [tasks]. Generate delegation prompts." → output ready-to-use prompts for each agent → AskUserQuestion (start / reorder / skip). 3) "PHASE 3: Execute" → present first task prompt → user invokes agent → user reports back → mark complete → present next. CRITICAL: Never skip user approval between phases. Use TodoWrite to track progress across agent switches.
